<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="tr">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>İngilizce Atama Simülasyonu</title>
<script>(function(){try{if(localStorage.getItem('atama_theme')==='dark'){document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme','dark');}}catch(e){}})();</script>
<link rel="icon" type="image/png" href="images/logo.png">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Newsreader:ital,opsz,wght@0,6..72,400;0,6..72,500;0,6..72,600;0,6..72,700;1,6..72,500&family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700&family=IBM+Plex+Mono:wght@400;500;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@supabase/supabase-js@2/dist/umd/supabase.min.js"></script>
<style>
  :root{
    --paper:#F4F5FB;
    --paper-dim:#E8EAF7;
    --surface:#FFFFFF;
    --ink:#1B1A3B;
    --text:#1B1A3B;
    --ink-soft:#57547E;
    --seal:#0D9488;
    --seal-dark:#0B7A70;
    --accent-blue:#2563EB;
    --green:#15803D;
    --line: rgba(27,26,59,0.13);
    --shadow: 0 1px 0 rgba(27,26,59,0.06);
    color-scheme: light;
  }
  html[data-theme="dark"]{
    --paper:#151515;
    --paper-dim:#1B1B1B;
    --surface:#202020;
    --ink:#3A3A3A;
    --text:#ECECEC;
    --ink-soft:#9E9E9E;
    --seal:#2DD4BF;
    --seal-dark:#14B8A6;
    --accent-blue:#60A5FA;
    --green:#4ADE80;
    --line: rgba(255,255,255,0.10);
    --shadow: 0 1px 0 rgba(0,0,0,0.4);
    color-scheme: dark;
  }
  html[data-theme="dark"] .map-legend i.off{background:#3A3A3A;}
  html[data-theme="dark"] .tr-map-svg-wrap path.il-disabled{fill:#3A3A3A;}
  html[data-theme="dark"] .tr-map-svg-wrap path.il-active{fill:#3E5C96;}
  html[data-theme="dark"] .tr-map-svg-wrap path.il-active:hover{fill:#4E78C4;}
  html[data-theme="dark"] .modal-overlay{background:rgba(0,0,0,0.65);}
  html[data-theme="dark"] .btn:hover{background:#2C2C2C;}
  html[data-theme="dark"] body{
    background:
      radial-gradient(ellipse at top left, rgba(255,255,255,0.03), transparent 45%),
      radial-gradient(ellipse at bottom right, rgba(45,212,191,0.05), transparent 50%),
      var(--paper);
  }
  html[data-theme="dark"] table.results tr.me{background:rgba(96,165,250,0.14);}
  html[data-theme="dark"] .modal-box{box-shadow:0 20px 60px -12px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);}
  html[data-theme="dark"] .theme-toggle-btn .icon-moon{display:none;}
  html:not([data-theme="dark"]) .theme-toggle-btn .icon-sun{display:none;}

  .theme-toggle-btn{
    position:fixed;top:20px;right:20px;z-index:60;
    width:42px;height:42px;border-radius:50%;
    border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--surface);color:var(--text);
    font-size:18px;line-height:1;cursor:pointer;
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
    box-shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(27,26,59,0.15);
    transition:background .2s ease,color .2s ease,border-color .2s ease,transform .15s ease;
  }
  .theme-toggle-btn:hover{transform:scale(1.07);}
  .theme-toggle-btn:active{transform:scale(.94);}
  @media (max-width:640px){
    .theme-toggle-btn{top:14px;right:14px;width:38px;height:38px;font-size:15px;}
  }

  *{box-sizing:border-box;}
  html{min-height:100%;background:var(--paper);}
  body{
    margin:0;
    min-height:100vh;
    display:flex;
    flex-direction:column;
    background:
      radial-gradient(ellipse at top left, rgba(124,58,237,0.08), transparent 45%),
      radial-gradient(ellipse at bottom right, rgba(13,148,136,0.06), transparent 50%),
      var(--paper);
    color:var(--text);
    font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;
    -webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased;
    transition:background .2s ease,color .2s ease;
  }
  .wrap{
    max-width:880px;margin:0 auto;padding:28px 20px 40px;
    width:100%;flex:1;display:flex;flex-direction:column;
  }

  /* header */
  .masthead{
    display:flex;align-items:flex-end;justify-content:space-between;
    border-bottom:2px solid var(--text);
    padding-bottom:14px;margin-bottom:6px;
    flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px;
  }
  .masthead-left .eyebrow{
    font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;
    font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;
    color:var(--seal);font-weight:600;
  }
  .masthead-left h1{
    font-family:'Newsreader',serif;
    font-weight:600;font-size:34px;margin:2px 0 0;line-height:1.05;
    letter-spacing:-0.01em;
  }
  .masthead-right{
    font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-soft);
    text-align:right;line-height:1.5;
  }
  .rule-sub{
    font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-soft);margin:8px 0 26px;
    font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;
  }

  /* stepper */
  .stepper{display:flex;gap:0;margin-bottom:28px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:2px;overflow:hidden;}
  .step-tab{
    flex:1;padding:12px 14px;background:var(--paper);cursor:pointer;
    border-right:1px solid var(--line);position:relative;
    transition:background .15s ease;
  }
  .step-tab:last-child{border-right:none;}
  .step-tab .num{
    font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-soft);
  }
  .step-tab .label{font-size:13px;font-weight:600;margin-top:2px;}
  .step-tab.active{background:var(--ink);}
  .step-tab.active .num{color:rgba(255,255,255,.55);}
  .step-tab.active .label{color:#fff;}
  .step-tab.done .num{color:var(--green);}
  .step-tab.locked{opacity:.45;cursor:not-allowed;}

  .panel{display:none;}
  .panel.active{display:block;animation:fadeUp .35s ease both;}
  @keyframes fadeUp{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(6px);}to{opacity:1;transform:none;}}

  .card{
    background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:3px;
    padding:26px;box-shadow:var(--shadow);margin-bottom:18px;
    transition:background .2s ease,border-color .2s ease;
  }
  #simIntroCard{
    overflow:hidden;max-height:600px;
    transition:opacity .35s ease, max-height .35s ease, padding .35s ease, margin .35s ease, border-color .35s ease;
  }
  #simIntroCard.collapsing{
    opacity:0;max-height:0;padding-top:0;padding-bottom:0;margin-bottom:0;border-color:transparent;
  }
  h2.section-title{
    font-family:'Newsreader',serif;font-weight:600;font-size:22px;margin:0 0 4px;
  }
  .section-desc{color:var(--ink-soft);font-size:14px;margin:0 0 20px;line-height:1.6;}

  label.field-label{display:block;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;color:var(--ink-soft);margin-bottom:6px;}
  input[type=text], input[type=number]{
    width:100%;padding:11px 12px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:2px;
    font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;font-size:15px;background:var(--paper);color:var(--text);
  }
  input:focus{outline:2px solid var(--accent-blue);outline-offset:0;background:var(--surface);}
  .field-row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:16px;margin-bottom:16px;}
  .field-row.three{grid-template-columns:2fr 1fr 1fr 90px;}
  @media (max-width:640px){.field-row,.field-row.three{grid-template-columns:1fr;}}

  .btn{
    font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;font-weight:600;font-size:14px;
    padding:12px 22px;border-radius:2px;border:1px solid var(--ink);
    background:var(--ink);color:#fff;cursor:pointer;transition:transform .1s ease, background .15s ease;
  }
  .btn:hover{background:#0f1830;}
  .btn:active{transform:scale(.98);}
  .btn.secondary{background:transparent;color:var(--text);}
  .btn.secondary:hover{background:var(--paper-dim);}
  .btn.seal-btn{background:var(--seal);border-color:var(--seal);}
  .btn.seal-btn:hover{background:var(--seal-dark);}
  .btn:disabled{opacity:.4;cursor:not-allowed;}
  .btn-sm{padding:6px 10px;font-size:12px;border-radius:2px;}
  #runSimBtn{min-width:246px;}
  @media (max-width:520px){#runSimBtn{min-width:0;}}


  /* year toggle */
  .year-toggle{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;margin-bottom:12px;}
  .year-toggle button:nth-child(3){grid-column:1 / -1;width:50%;justify-self:center;}
  @media (max-width:640px){
    .year-toggle button:nth-child(3){grid-column:auto;width:100%;}
  }
  .year-toggle button{
    padding:11px 12px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:2px;
    font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;font-weight:600;font-size:14px;
    background:var(--paper);color:var(--ink-soft);cursor:pointer;
    transition:background .15s ease,color .15s ease,border-color .15s ease;
  }
  .year-toggle button:hover{background:var(--paper-dim);}
  .year-toggle button.active{background:var(--ink);border-color:var(--ink);color:#fff;}

  /* school list */
  .school-filter-row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;margin-bottom:12px;}
  @media (max-width:640px){.school-filter-row{grid-template-columns:1fr;}}
  select.filter-select{
    width:100%;padding:11px 12px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:2px;
    font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;font-size:14px;background:var(--paper);color:var(--text);
    cursor:pointer;
  }
  select.filter-select:disabled{opacity:.5;cursor:not-allowed;}
  select.filter-select:focus{outline:2px solid var(--accent-blue);outline-offset:0;background:var(--surface);}
  /* map picker */
  .map-toggle-row{display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin:-4px 0 12px;}
  .map-toggle-btn{
    font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-weight:600;font-size:12px;letter-spacing:.03em;
    padding:9px 14px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:2px;background:var(--paper);
    color:var(--ink-soft);cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;
    transition:background .15s ease,color .15s ease,border-color .15s ease;
  }
  .map-toggle-btn:hover{background:var(--paper-dim);color:var(--text);}
  .map-toggle-btn.active{background:var(--ink);border-color:var(--ink);color:#fff;}
  .map-toggle-btn .chevron{transition:transform .15s ease;font-size:10px;}
  .map-toggle-btn.active .chevron{transform:rotate(180deg);}

  .map-panel{
    display:none;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:3px;background:var(--paper);
    padding:16px 16px 12px;margin-bottom:16px;
  }
  .map-panel.open{display:block;animation:fadeUp .3s ease both;}
  .map-panel-head{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;margin-bottom:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;}
  .map-panel-title{font-size:12px;font-weight:600;color:var(--ink-soft);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;}
  .map-legend{display:flex;gap:14px;font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-soft);}
  .map-legend span{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;}
  .map-legend i{width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:2px;display:inline-block;}
  .map-legend i.on{background:var(--accent-blue);}
  .map-legend i.off{background:#c7c9d9;}

  .tr-map-svg-wrap{position:relative;width:100%;max-width:620px;margin:0 auto;}
  .tr-map-svg-wrap svg{width:100%;height:auto;display:block;}
  .tr-map-svg-wrap path{stroke:#fff;stroke-width:1.1;transition:fill .12s ease,opacity .12s ease;}
  .tr-map-svg-wrap path.il-active{fill:#93b7f5;cursor:pointer;}
  .tr-map-svg-wrap path.il-active:hover{fill:var(--accent-blue);}
  .tr-map-svg-wrap path.il-selected{fill:var(--seal)!important;}
  .tr-map-svg-wrap path.il-disabled{fill:#c7c9d9;cursor:not-allowed;}
  .map-hint{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--ink-soft);text-align:center;margin-top:8px;font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;}
  .map-il-tooltip{
    position:absolute;pointer-events:none;z-index:50;background:var(--ink);color:#fff;
    font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;padding:5px 9px;border-radius:2px;
    transform:translate(-50%,-130%);white-space:nowrap;display:none;left:0;top:0;
  }

  .school-search{margin-bottom:14px;}
  .school-list{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:2px;max-height:360px;overflow-y:auto;}
  .school-row{
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;
    padding:12px 14px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);
  }
  .school-row:last-child{border-bottom:none;}
  .school-main{font-size:14px;font-weight:600;}
  .school-meta{font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-soft);margin-top:2px;font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;}
  .quota-pill{
    font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:11px;color:var(--green);
    border:1px solid var(--green);border-radius:20px;padding:2px 8px;
  }

  .pref-list{list-style:none;margin:0;padding:0;}
  .pref-item{
    display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 12px;
    border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:2px;margin-bottom:8px;background:var(--paper);
    cursor:grab;transition:background .12s ease,border-color .12s ease;
  }
  .pref-item:hover{background:var(--surface);border-color:var(--accent-blue);}
  .pref-item.dragging{opacity:.5;cursor:grabbing;}
  .pref-item.drag-over{border-top:2px solid var(--accent-blue);}
  .pref-order{
    font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-weight:600;color:#fff;background:var(--ink);
    width:26px;height:26px;border-radius:50%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
    font-size:12px;flex-shrink:0;
  }
  .pref-name{flex:1;font-size:13.5px;font-weight:600;}
  .pref-meta{font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-soft);font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;}
  .pref-actions{display:flex;gap:4px;}
  .icon-btn{
    background:none;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:2px;width:26px;height:26px;
    cursor:pointer;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink-soft);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
  }
  .icon-btn:hover{background:var(--paper-dim);color:var(--text);}

  .empty-note{
    font-size:13px;color:var(--ink-soft);text-align:center;padding:24px;border:1px dashed var(--line);border-radius:2px;
  }

  .counter-badge{
    font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-soft);
    display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-bottom:16px;
  }
  .dot{width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--green);}

  .sim-actions-bar{
    display:none;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:14px;flex-wrap:wrap;
    background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:3px;
    padding:12px 16px;margin-bottom:24px;
    position:sticky;top:12px;z-index:5;box-shadow:var(--shadow);
  }
  .sim-actions-bar.show{display:flex;animation:fadeUp .35s ease both;}
  .sim-actions-bar .counter-badge{margin-bottom:0;}
  .sim-actions-bar .sim-actions-buttons{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;}
  .sim-actions-bar .sim-actions-buttons #backToStep1{margin-left:0;}
  @media (max-width:640px){
    .sim-actions-bar{position:static;}
  }

  table.results{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:13px;}
  table.results th{
    text-align:left;font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:10.5px;text-transform:uppercase;
    letter-spacing:.05em;color:var(--ink-soft);border-bottom:1px solid var(--text);padding:8px 10px;
  }
  table.results td{padding:9px 10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);}
  table.results tr.me{background:rgba(37,99,235,0.08);}
  table.results tr.me td{font-weight:700;}
  .placed{color:var(--green);font-weight:600;}
  .unplaced{color:var(--ink-soft);font-weight:600;}
  .placed-city{display:block;color:var(--ink-soft);font-weight:400;font-size:10.5px;font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;margin-top:2px;}

  .disclosure{
    font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-soft);background:var(--paper-dim);border:1px solid var(--line);
    border-radius:2px;padding:12px 14px;margin-top:16px;line-height:1.6;
  }
  .toast{
    position:fixed;bottom:24px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);
    background:var(--ink);color:#fff;padding:11px 18px;border-radius:3px;font-size:13px;
    font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;opacity:0;pointer-events:none;transition:opacity .25s ease, transform .25s ease;
    z-index:50;
  }
  .toast.show{opacity:1;transform:translateX(-50%) translateY(-6px);}

  /* modal */
  .modal-overlay{
    position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(27,26,59,0.55);
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:20px;
    z-index:100;opacity:0;pointer-events:none;transition:opacity .2s ease;
  }
  .modal-overlay.show{opacity:1;pointer-events:auto;}
  .modal-box{
    background:var(--surface);border-radius:4px;max-width:480px;width:100%;
    padding:26px 26px 22px;box-shadow:0 20px 60px -12px rgba(27,26,59,0.35);
    transform:translateY(8px);transition:transform .2s ease;
  }
  .modal-overlay.show .modal-box{transform:translateY(0);}
  .modal-title{font-family:'Newsreader',serif;font-weight:600;font-size:19px;margin:0 0 12px;color:var(--text);}
  .modal-body{font-size:14px;color:var(--ink-soft);line-height:1.7;margin-bottom:18px;white-space:pre-line;}
  .modal-body .law-note{
    display:block;margin-top:10px;font-size:12.5px;color:var(--text);
    background:var(--paper-dim);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:2px;padding:10px 12px;
  }
  .modal-actions{display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;gap:10px;}
  .row-between{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:20px;margin-bottom:28px;}
  .hint{font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-soft);}
  .result-table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:2px;overflow:hidden;margin-top:6px;table-layout:fixed;}
  .result-table caption{
    background:var(--green);color:#fff;font-weight:700;font-size:14px;
    padding:12px 14px;text-align:center;caption-side:top;font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;
  }
  .result-table.unplaced caption{background:var(--ink-soft);}
  .result-table td{padding:13px 16px;border-top:1px solid var(--line);font-size:14.5px;word-break:break-word;}
  .result-table td.rlabel{font-weight:700;width:130px;background:var(--paper-dim);white-space:nowrap;}
  .result-table td.rvalue{color:var(--green);font-weight:600;}
  .result-table.unplaced td.rvalue{color:var(--ink-soft);}
  @media (max-width:640px){
    .result-table td.rlabel{width:110px;}
  }

  /* rank banner */
  .rank-banner{
    display:flex;align-items:center;gap:20px;margin-top:18px;
    padding:18px 22px;border-radius:8px;
    background:var(--paper-dim);border:1px solid var(--line);
    border-left:4px solid var(--seal);
  }
  .rank-badge{
    flex-shrink:0;width:60px;height:60px;border-radius:50%;
    background:var(--seal-dark);
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
    font-family:'Newsreader',serif;font-weight:700;font-size:24px;color:#fff;
  }
  .rank-text{flex:1;min-width:0;}
  .rank-headline{font-size:15px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text);line-height:1.4;}
  .rank-headline .hl{color:var(--seal-dark);}
  .rank-sub{font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--ink-soft);margin-top:4px;letter-spacing:.02em;}
  .result-note{font-size:13px;color:var(--ink-soft);margin-top:14px;line-height:1.6;}
  .footer-credit{
    text-align:center;margin-top:auto;padding-top:18px;
    padding-top:46px;padding-bottom:4px;border-top:1px solid var(--line);
    font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-soft);
    letter-spacing:.02em;
  }
  .footer-credit .heart{display:inline-block;animation:beat 1.6s ease-in-out infinite;}
  .footer-contact{margin-top:6px;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--ink-soft);}
  .footer-contact a{color:var(--ink-soft);text-decoration:none;border-bottom:1px dotted var(--line);}
  .footer-contact a:hover{color:var(--seal);border-bottom-color:var(--seal);}
  @keyframes beat{0%,100%{transform:scale(1);}25%{transform:scale(1.18);}}

  /* score preview box (Step 1) */
  .score-preview{
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:16px;
    background:var(--paper-dim);border:1px solid var(--line);border-left:4px solid var(--accent-blue);
    border-radius:2px;padding:16px 18px;margin:6px 0 18px;
  }
  .score-preview-label{
    font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;
    letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:4px;
  }
  .score-preview-sub{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink-soft);}
  .score-preview-value{
    font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;font-weight:700;font-size:28px;color:var(--accent-blue);
    flex-shrink:0;white-space:nowrap;
  }
</style>
</head>
<body>
<button type="button" class="theme-toggle-btn" id="themeToggleBtn" title="Gündüz / Gece modu" aria-label="Tema değiştir">
  <span class="icon-moon">🌙</span><span class="icon-sun">☀️</span>
</button>
<div class="wrap">

  <div class="masthead">
    <div class="masthead-left">
      <h1>İngilizce Atama Simülasyonu</h1>
    </div>
    <div class="masthead-right" id="poolInfo">Havuz yükleniyor…</div>
  </div>
  <div class="rule-sub">Puanınızı girin, sıralamanızı görün, 40 okul seçin — diğer katılımcıların tercihleriyle birlikte olası yerleşiminizi tahmin edin.</div>

  <div class="stepper" id="stepper"></div>

  <!-- STEP 1: SCORE -->
  <div class="panel" id="panel-0">
    <div class="card">
      <h2 class="section-title">1 · Ortalamalarınızı girin</h2>
      <p class="section-desc">Teorik dersler ortalamanızın %40'ı ile uygulamalı dersler ortalamanızın %60'ı toplanarak <b>atamaya esas puanınız</b> hesaplanır. Bu puan girildiği anda, o ana kadar sisteme giren herkesle karşılaştırılır ve size sıralamanızı gösteririz. Rumuz isteğe bağlıdır; sonuç tablosunda gerçek adınız yerine bunu kullanabilirsiniz.</p>
      <div class="field-row">
        <div>
          <label class="field-label">Teorik Dersler Ortalaması (%40)</label>
          <input type="number" id="theoreticalInput" placeholder="örn. 78.500" step="0.001" min="60" max="100">
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="field-label">Uygulamalı Dersler Ortalaması (%60)</label>
          <input type="number" id="practicalInput" placeholder="örn. 86.250" step="0.001" min="70" max="100">
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="field-row">
        <div>
          <label class="field-label">Rumuz (opsiyonel)</label>
          <input type="text" id="nameInput" placeholder="örn. GaziEgitim-1" maxlength="30">
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-soft);font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;margin:-8px 0 4px;">Atamaya Esas Puan = (Teorik Ortalama × %40) + (Uygulamalı Ortalama × %60)</div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-soft);font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;margin:0 0 16px;">Teorik ortalama en az 60, uygulamalı ortalama en az 70 olmalıdır.</div>
      <div class="score-preview">
        <div>
          <div class="score-preview-label">Atamaya Esas Puanınız</div>
          <div class="score-preview-sub" id="computedScoreSub">Teorik ve uygulamalı ortalamalarınızı girin</div>
        </div>
        <div class="score-preview-value" id="computedScoreDisplay">—</div>
      </div>
      <button class="btn seal-btn" id="saveScoreBtn">Puanı Kaydet ve Sıramı Gör</button>

      <div id="rankZone"></div>

      <div class="disclosure">Puanınız ve seçtiğiniz rumuz, bu simülasyona katılan herkese açık ortak bir havuzda tutulur — gerçek adınızı kullanmamanızı öneririz. Bu araç MEB'in resmi atama sistemi değildir, yalnızca aynı siteyi kullanan kişilerin verileriyle çalışan bir tahmin denemesidir.</div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- STEP 2: SCHOOLS -->
  <div class="panel" id="panel-1">
    <div class="card">
      <h2 class="section-title">2 · Okul tercihlerinizi oluşturun</h2>
      <p class="section-desc">Havuzda yıllara göre İngilizce boş kadro listeleri hazır yüklü. Aşağıdan yılı seçin, arama yapıp en fazla 40 tercih ekleyin ve sırasını düzenleyin — herkes aynı havuzu kullanır.</p>

      <div class="year-toggle">
        <button type="button" id="yearToggle2024" class="active">2024 Okulları</button>
        <button type="button" id="yearToggle2023">2023 Okulları</button>
         <button type="button" id="yearToggle2022">2022 Okulları</button>
      </div>

      <div class="school-filter-row">
        <select class="filter-select" id="ilSelect">
          <option value="">Tüm İller</option>
        </select>
        <select class="filter-select" id="ilceSelect" disabled>
          <option value="">Tüm İlçeler</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <div class="map-toggle-row">
        <button type="button" class="map-toggle-btn" id="mapToggleBtn">🗺️ Haritadan Seç <span class="chevron">▾</span></button>
      </div>
      <div class="map-panel" id="mapPanel">
        <div class="map-panel-head">
          <div class="map-panel-title">İl seçmek için haritaya tıklayın</div>
          <div class="map-legend">
            <span><i class="on"></i> Kontenjan açık</span>
            <span><i class="off"></i> Kontenjan yok</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="tr-map-svg-wrap" id="trMapSvgWrap">
          <div class="map-il-tooltip" id="mapIlTooltip"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="map-hint">Mavi iller tıklanabilir · gri illerde bu yıl açık kadro bulunmuyor</div>
      </div>
      <input type="text" class="school-search" id="schoolSearch" placeholder="Okul, ilçe/il veya branş ara…">
      <div class="school-list" id="schoolList"></div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <h2 class="section-title">Tercih sıranız <span id="prefCount" style="color:var(--ink-soft);font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:14px;"></span></h2>
      <p class="section-desc">1. sıradaki, en çok istediğiniz yer olmalı. Simülasyon bu sırayı esas alır.</p>
      <ul class="pref-list" id="prefList"></ul>
      <div id="prefEmpty" class="empty-note">Henüz tercih eklemediniz. Yukarıdaki listeden "Tercih Et" ile ekleyin.</div>
    </div>

    <div class="row-between">
      <button class="btn secondary" id="backToStep0">← Puan Ekranına Dön</button>
      <button class="btn seal-btn" id="toSimBtn">Tercihleri Kaydet, Simülasyona Geç →</button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- STEP 3: SIMULATE -->
  <div class="panel" id="panel-2">
    <div class="card" id="simIntroCard">
      <h2 class="section-title">3 · Simülasyonu başlat</h2>
      <p class="section-desc">Simülasyon; havuzdaki tüm katılımcıları puana göre yüksekten düşüğe sıralar, sırayla herkesi kendi tercih listesinde kontenjanı boş olan ilk okula yerleştirir — atama sisteminin mantığına en yakın deneme budur. Katılımcı sayısı arttıkça tahmin gerçeğe yaklaşır.</p>
      <div class="counter-badge" id="simCounter"><span class="dot"></span> Havuz durumu yükleniyor…</div>
      <button class="btn seal-btn" id="runSimBtn">Simülasyonu Başlat</button>
      <button class="btn secondary" id="backToStep1" style="margin-left:8px;">← Tercihleri Düzenle</button>
    </div>

    <div class="sim-actions-bar" id="simActionsBar"></div>

    <div class="card" id="myResultCard" style="display:none;"></div>

    <div class="card" id="tableCard" style="display:none;">
      <h2 class="section-title">Tüm katılımcılar</h2>
      <p class="section-desc">Puana göre sıralı, bu denemedeki tahmini yerleşim sonuçları.</p>
      <div style="overflow-x:auto;">
        <table class="results" id="resultsTable"></table>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="footer-credit">
    Designed by Tayfur with <span class="heart">❤️</span>
    <div class="footer-contact">For all inquiries, please contact me at <a href="mailto:tayfurr@protonmail.com">tayfurr@protonmail.com</a></div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="toast" id="toast"></div>

<div class="modal-overlay" id="modalOverlay">
  <div class="modal-box">
    <h3 class="modal-title" id="modalTitle"></h3>
    <div class="modal-body" id="modalBody"></div>
    <div class="modal-actions">
      <button class="btn seal-btn" id="modalOkBtn">Anladım</button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

  const $ = (id)=>document.getElementById(id);

  // ---------- THEME (gündüz/gece modu) ----------
  function applyTheme(theme){
    if (theme === 'dark'){ document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme','dark'); }
    else { document.documentElement.removeAttribute('data-theme'); }
    try{ localStorage.setItem('atama_theme', theme); }catch(e){}
  }
  (function initTheme(){
    let saved = null;
    try{ saved = localStorage.getItem('atama_theme'); }catch(e){}
    applyTheme(saved === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light');
  })();
  const themeBtn = $('themeToggleBtn');
  if (themeBtn){
    themeBtn.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
      const isDark = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark';
      applyTheme(isDark ? 'light' : 'dark');
    });
  }

  function toast(msg){
    const t = $('toast'); t.textContent = msg; t.classList.add('show');
    clearTimeout(t._h); t._h = setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'), 2200);
  }
  function showModal(title, bodyHtml){
    $('modalTitle').textContent = title;
    $('modalBody').innerHTML = bodyHtml;
    $('modalOverlay').classList.add('show');
  }
  function hideModal(){
    $('modalOverlay').classList.remove('show');
  }
  $('modalOkBtn').onclick = hideModal;
  $('modalOverlay').addEventListener('click', (e)=>{ if (e.target.id === 'modalOverlay') hideModal(); });

"hizmet": "3/6"}];

  // Kişisel veriler (profil, tercih sırası) bu tarayıcıda localStorage'da tutulur.
  // Havuz verileri (okullar, katılımcılar) Supabase'deki paylaşımlı tablolarda tutulur.
  function safeGetLocal(key){
    try{ return localStorage.getItem(key); }
    catch(e){ return null; }
  }
  function safeSetLocal(key, value){
    try{ localStorage.setItem(key, value); return true; }
    catch(e){ console.error('localStorage set failed', key, e); return null; }
  }

  async function fetchSchools(){
    if (!sb) return [];
    const PAGE_SIZE = 1000; // Supabase varsayılan tek sorgu üst limiti
    let all = [];
    let from = 0;
    while (true){
      const { data, error } = await sb.from('schools').select('*')
        .order('city', { ascending: true })
        .range(from, from + PAGE_SIZE - 1);
      if (error){ console.error('schools fetch failed', error); break; }
      if (!data || data.length === 0) break;
      all = all.concat(data);
      if (data.length < PAGE_SIZE) break; // son sayfa
      from += PAGE_SIZE;
    }
    return all;
  }

  async function seedSchools(){
    if (!sb) return [];
    const rows = SEED_SCHOOLS.map(s => ({
      name: s.name, city: s.city, branch: s.branch,
      quota: s.quota, kod: s.kod || null, hizmet: s.hizmet || null, yil: 2024
    }));
    const { data, error } = await sb.from('schools').insert(rows).select();
    if (error){ console.error('schools seed failed', error); return []; }
    return data || [];
  }

  async function fetchParticipants(){
    if (!sb) return [];
    const PAGE_SIZE = 1000;
    let all = [];
    let from = 0;
    while (true){
      const { data, error } = await sb.from('participants').select('*').range(from, from + PAGE_SIZE - 1);
      if (error){ console.error('participants fetch failed', error); break; }
      if (!data || data.length === 0) break;
      all = all.concat(data);
      if (data.length < PAGE_SIZE) break;
      from += PAGE_SIZE;
    }
    return all.map(p => ({
      id: p.id, name: p.name, score: Number(p.score),
      preferences: p.preferences || [],
      updatedAt: p.updated_at ? new Date(p.updated_at).getTime() : 0
    }));
  }

  async function upsertParticipant(p){
    if (!sb) return null;
    const { error } = await sb.from('participants').upsert({
      id: p.id, name: p.name, score: p.score,
      preferences: p.preferences, updated_at: new Date(p.updatedAt || Date.now()).toISOString()
    });
    if (error){ console.error('participant upsert failed', error); toast('Puanınız havuza kaydedilemedi.'); return null; }
    return true;
  }

  function fmtScore(s){
    if (s === null || s === undefined || isNaN(s)) return '-';
    return Number(s).toLocaleString('tr-TR', {maximumFractionDigits:3});
  }

  // ---------- INIT ----------
  async function init(){
    renderStepper();

    if (!sb){
      $('poolInfo').textContent = 'Supabase bağlantısı yapılandırılmadı';
      toast('Lütfen script içindeki SUPABASE_URL / SUPABASE_ANON_KEY değerlerini girin.');
    }

    // personal profile (bu tarayıcıya özel, localStorage)
    const rawProfile = safeGetLocal('my-profile');
    if (rawProfile){
      try{
        const p = JSON.parse(rawProfile);
        state.profileId = p.id;
        state.profile = { name: p.name || '', score: p.score ?? null };
        $('theoreticalInput').value = p.theoretical ?? '';
        $('practicalInput').value = p.practical ?? '';
        $('nameInput').value = p.name || '';
        updateComputedScoreDisplay();
      }catch(e){}
    }
    if (!state.profileId) state.profileId = uid();

    const rawPrefs = safeGetLocal('my-preferences');
    if (rawPrefs){
      try{ state.myPrefs = JSON.parse(rawPrefs).schoolIds || []; }catch(e){ state.myPrefs = []; }
    }

    // paylaşımlı okul havuzu (Supabase)
    let schools = await fetchSchools();
    if (schools.length === 0){
      schools = await seedSchools();
    }
    state.schools = schools;

    if (state.profile.score !== null) {
      state.maxStepUnlocked = Math.max(state.maxStepUnlocked, 1);
      await renderRankZone();
    }
    if (state.myPrefs.length > 0){
      state.maxStepUnlocked = Math.max(state.maxStepUnlocked, 2);
    }

    renderIlSelect();
    renderSchoolList();
    renderPrefList();
    updateMapSelection();
    await updatePoolInfo();
    goStep(0, true);

    showModal('Hoş Geldiniz', `Kıymetli öğretmenlerim, öncelikle hepimize geçmiş olsun. Zorlu bir süreci atlattık. Şimdi tercih dönemindeyiz. Sıralamamızı öğrenebilmek ve sağlıklı tercih süreci yürütebilmek için sizlere şu şekilde site tasarladık. Kullanımı tamamen ücretsizdir. Lütfen meslektaşlarımızla paylaşalım

En önemlisi, puan verilerimizi lütfen doğru girelim.`);
  }

  // ---------- STEPPER ----------
  function renderStepper(){
    const el = $('stepper');
    el.innerHTML = '';
    STEP_LABELS.forEach((s, i)=>{
      const d = document.createElement('div');
      d.className = 'step-tab' + (i===state.step ? ' active':'') + (i < state.step ? ' done':'') + (i > state.maxStepUnlocked ? ' locked':'');
      d.innerHTML = `<div class="num">${s.n}</div><div class="label">${s.l}</div>`;
      d.onclick = ()=>{ if (i <= state.maxStepUnlocked) goStep(i); };
      el.appendChild(d);
    });
  }
  function goStep(i, silent){
    state.step = i;
    document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach((p,idx)=>{ p.classList.toggle('active', idx===i); });
    renderStepper();
    if (!silent) window.scrollTo({top:0, behavior:'smooth'});
    if (i === 2) refreshSimCounter();
  }

  // ---------- POOL INFO ----------
  async function getAllParticipants(){
    const out = await fetchParticipants();
    // dedupe by id, just in case
    const map = {};
    out.forEach(p => { map[p.id] = p; });
    return Object.values(map);
  }

  async function updatePoolInfo(){
    const all = await getAllParticipants();
    $('poolInfo').textContent = `Havuzda ${all.length} katılımcı`;
  }

  async function refreshSimCounter(){
    const all = await getAllParticipants();
    const totalQuota = state.schools.reduce((a,s)=>a+(Number(s.quota)||0),0);
    $('simCounter').innerHTML = `<span class="dot"></span> ${all.length} katılımcı · ${state.schools.length} okul · toplam ${totalQuota} kontenjan havuzda`;
  }

  // ---------- STEP 1: SCORE / RANK ----------
  function computeAssignmentScore(theoretical, practical){
    if (isNaN(theoretical) || isNaN(practical)) return null;
    return (theoretical * 0.4) + (practical * 0.6);
  }

  function updateComputedScoreDisplay(){
    const t = parseFloat($('theoreticalInput').value);
    const p = parseFloat($('practicalInput').value);
    const computed = computeAssignmentScore(t, p);
    $('computedScoreDisplay').textContent = computed === null ? '—' : fmtScore(computed);
    $('computedScoreSub').textContent = computed === null
      ? 'Teorik ve uygulamalı ortalamalarınızı girin'
      : 'Otomatik hesaplandı';
  }
  $('theoreticalInput').addEventListener('input', updateComputedScoreDisplay);
  $('practicalInput').addEventListener('input', updateComputedScoreDisplay);

  $('saveScoreBtn').onclick = async ()=>{
    const theoretical = parseFloat($('theoreticalInput').value);
    const practical = parseFloat($('practicalInput').value);
    if (isNaN(theoretical) || isNaN(practical)){ toast('Lütfen her iki ortalamayı da girin.'); return; }
    if (theoretical < 60 || theoretical > 100){ toast('Teorik dersler ortalaması 60 ile 100 arasında olmalıdır.'); return; }
    if (practical < 70 || practical > 100){ toast('Uygulamalı dersler ortalaması 70 ile 100 arasında olmalıdır.'); return; }
    const score = computeAssignmentScore(theoretical, practical);
    const name = $('nameInput').value.trim();
    state.profile = { name, score };
    updateComputedScoreDisplay();

    safeSetLocal('my-profile', JSON.stringify({ id: state.profileId, name, theoretical, practical, score }));
    await upsertParticipant({
      id: state.profileId, name: name || ('Aday-'+state.profileId.slice(0,4)),
      score, preferences: state.myPrefs, updatedAt: Date.now()
    });

    state.maxStepUnlocked = Math.max(state.maxStepUnlocked, 1);
    renderStepper();
    await renderRankZone();
    await updatePoolInfo();
    toast('Puanınız kaydedildi.');

    const allAfter = await getAllParticipants();
    const hasTie = allAfter.some(p => p.id !== state.profileId && Number(p.score) === Number(score));
    if (hasTie){
      showModal('Puan Eşitliği Uyarısı', `
        Sizinle aynı puana sahip bir öğretmen daha var.
        <span class="law-note">"Atamaya esas başarı puanının eşitliği hâlinde MEB-AGS puanı yüksek olana öncelik verilir. Eşitliğin bu şekilde bozulmaması durumunda yerleştirilecek aday bilgisayar kurası ile belirlenir." 7528 Sayılı Kanun</span>
      `);
    }
  };

  async function renderRankZone(){
    const zone = $('rankZone');
    if (state.profile.score === null){ zone.innerHTML=''; return; }
    const all = await getAllParticipants();
    const higher = all.filter(p => p.score > state.profile.score && p.id !== state.profileId).length;
    const rank = higher + 1;
    zone.innerHTML = `
      <div class="rank-banner">
        <div class="rank-badge">${rank}.</div>
        <div class="rank-text">
          <div class="rank-headline"><span class="hl">${fmtScore(state.profile.score)}</span> puan ile havuzdaki <span class="hl">${all.length}</span> katılımcı arasında <span class="hl">${rank}.</span> sıradasınız.</div>
          <div class="rank-sub">PUAN ${fmtScore(state.profile.score)} · HAVUZ ${all.length} KİŞİ</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="row-between">
        <span></span>
        <button class="btn seal-btn" id="toStep2Btn">Okul Tercihine Geç →</button>
      </div>
    `;
    $('toStep2Btn').onclick = ()=>{ state.maxStepUnlocked = Math.max(state.maxStepUnlocked,1); renderStepper(); goStep(1); };
  }

  // ---------- STEP 2: SCHOOLS ----------

  const TURKEY_MAP_NAMES = {"ADA":"Adana","ADI":"Adıyaman","AFY":"Afyonkarahisar","AGR":"Ağrı","AKS":"Aksaray","AMA":"Amasya","ANK":"Ankara","ANT":"Antalya","ARD":"Ardahan","ART":"Artvin","AYD":"Aydın","BAL":"Balıkesir","BAR":"Bartın","BAT":"Batman","BAY":"Bayburt","BIL":"Bilecik","BIN":"Bingöl","BIT":"Bitlis","BOL":"Bolu","BRD":"Burdur","BUR":"Bursa","CAN":"Çankırı","CNK":"Çanakkale","COR":"Çorum","DEN":"Denizli","DIY":"Diyarbakır","DUZ":"Düzce","EDI":"Edirne","ELA":"Elazığ","ERC":"Erzincan","ERZ":"Erzurum","ESK":"Eskişehir","GAZ":"Gaziantep","GIR":"Giresun","GUM":"Gümüşhane","HAK":"Hakkari","HAT":"Hatay","IGD":"Iğdır","ISP":"Isparta","IST":"İstanbul","IZM":"İzmir","KAH":"Kahramanmaraş","KAR":"Kars","KAS":"Kastamonu","KAY":"Kayseri","KIL":"Kilis","KIR":"Kırklareli","KOC":"Kocaeli","KON":"Konya","KRB":"Karabük","KRK":"Kırıkkale","KRM":"Karaman","KRS":"Kırşehir","KUT":"Kütahya","MAL":"Malatya","MAN":"Manisa","MAR":"Mardin","MER":"Mersin","MUG":"Muğla","MUS":"Muş","NEV":"Nevşehir","NIG":"Niğde","ORD":"Ordu","OSM":"Osmaniye","RIZ":"Rize","SAK":"Sakarya","SAM":"Samsun","SAN":"Şanlıurfa","SII":"Siirt","SIN":"Sinop","SIR":"Şırnak","SIV":"Sivas","TEK":"Tekirdağ","TOK":"Tokat","TRA":"Trabzon","TUN":"Tunceli","USA":"Uşak","VAN":"Van","YAL":"Yalova","YOZ":"Yozgat","ZON":"Zonguldak"};
  function normIl(s){
    return (s||'')
      .toLocaleUpperCase('tr-TR')
      .replace(/İ/g,'I').replace(/I/g,'I')
      .replace(/[^A-ZÇĞİÖŞÜ ]/g,'')
      .trim();
  }
  // bazı olası isim farklılıklarını haritadaki resmi adlara eşle
  const IL_ALIASES = {
    'AFYON':'AFYONKARAHISAR','KMARAS':'KAHRAMANMARAS','MARAS':'KAHRAMANMARAS',
    'URFA':'SANLIURFA','ICEL':'MERSIN'
  };
  function resolveIlName(dataIlName, availableIlNames){
    const target = normIl(dataIlName);
    const targetResolved = IL_ALIASES[target] || target;
    return availableIlNames.find(n => {
      const cand = normIl(n);
      return cand === target || cand === targetResolved || (IL_ALIASES[cand]||cand) === targetResolved;
    });
  }

  let mapBuilt = false;
  function buildTrMapSvg(){
    if (mapBuilt) return;
    mapBuilt = true;
    const wrap = $('trMapSvgWrap');
    const svgHolder = document.createElement('div');
    const parts = ['<svg viewBox="0 20 1030 465" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">'];
    Object.keys(TURKEY_MAP_PATHS).forEach(code=>{
      const name = TURKEY_MAP_NAMES[code];
      parts.push(`<path data-code="${code}" data-il="${escapeHtml(name)}" d="${TURKEY_MAP_PATHS[code]}"></path>`);
    });
    parts.push('</svg>');
    svgHolder.innerHTML = parts.join('');
    wrap.insertBefore(svgHolder.firstChild, wrap.firstChild);
    const tooltip = $('mapIlTooltip');
    wrap.querySelectorAll('path').forEach(p=>{
      p.addEventListener('mousemove', (e)=>{
        const wrapRect = wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
        tooltip.style.display = 'block';
        tooltip.style.left = (e.clientX - wrapRect.left) + 'px';
        tooltip.style.top = (e.clientY - wrapRect.top) + 'px';
        tooltip.textContent = p.getAttribute('data-il');
      });
      p.addEventListener('mouseleave', ()=>{ tooltip.style.display = 'none'; });
      p.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
        if (!p.classList.contains('il-active')) return;
        const matched = resolveIlName(p.getAttribute('data-il'), getIlList());
        if (!matched) return;
        $('ilSelect').value = matched;
        renderIlceSelect(matched);
        renderSchoolList($('schoolSearch').value);
        updateMapSelection();
        toast(`${matched} seçildi`);
      });
    });
  }

  function updateMapSelection(){
    const wrap = $('trMapSvgWrap');
    if (!wrap || !wrap.firstChild) return;
    const activeIls = getIlList();
    const selected = $('ilSelect').value;
    wrap.querySelectorAll('path').forEach(p=>{
      const matched = resolveIlName(p.getAttribute('data-il'), activeIls);
      p.classList.toggle('il-active', !!matched);
      p.classList.toggle('il-disabled', !matched);
      p.classList.toggle('il-selected', !!matched && matched === selected);
    });
  }

  function toggleMapPanel(){
    const panel = $('mapPanel');
    const btn = $('mapToggleBtn');
    const opening = !panel.classList.contains('open');
    panel.classList.toggle('open', opening);
    btn.classList.toggle('active', opening);
    if (opening){
      buildTrMapSvg();
      updateMapSelection();
    }
  }
  $('mapToggleBtn').addEventListener('click', toggleMapPanel);

  function splitCity(city){
    const parts = (city || '').split('/');
    return { il: (parts[0] || '').trim(), ilce: (parts[1] || '').trim() };
  }

  function schoolsInSelectedYear(){
    return state.schools.filter(s => Number(s.yil || 2024) === state.schoolYearFilter);
  }

  function getIlList(){
    const set = new Set();
    schoolsInSelectedYear().forEach(s => { const { il } = splitCity(s.city); if (il) set.add(il); });
    return Array.from(set).sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'tr'));
  }

  function getIlceList(il){
    const set = new Set();
    schoolsInSelectedYear().forEach(s => {
      const c = splitCity(s.city);
      if (c.il === il && c.ilce) set.add(c.ilce);
    });
    return Array.from(set).sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'tr'));
  }

  function renderIlSelect(){
    const el = $('ilSelect');
    const current = el.value;
    const ils = getIlList();
    el.innerHTML = '<option value="">Tüm İller</option>' + ils.map(il => `<option value="${escapeHtml(il)}">${escapeHtml(il)}</option>`).join('');
    if (ils.includes(current)) el.value = current;
  }

  function renderIlceSelect(il){
    const el = $('ilceSelect');
    if (!il){
      el.innerHTML = '<option value="">Tüm İlçeler</option>';
      el.disabled = true;
      return;
    }
    el.disabled = false;
    const ilceler = getIlceList(il);
    el.innerHTML = '<option value="">Tüm İlçeler</option>' + ilceler.map(i => `<option value="${escapeHtml(i)}">${escapeHtml(i)}</option>`).join('');
  }

  function renderSchoolList(filter){
    const el = $('schoolList');
    const f = (filter||'').toLocaleLowerCase('tr-TR');
    const selectedIl = $('ilSelect').value;
    const selectedIlce = $('ilceSelect').value;
    const list = schoolsInSelectedYear().filter(s=>{
      if (selectedIl){
        const c = splitCity(s.city);
        if (c.il !== selectedIl) return false;
        if (selectedIlce && c.ilce !== selectedIlce) return false;
      }
      if (!f) return true;
      return (s.name+' '+s.city+' '+s.branch).toLocaleLowerCase('tr-TR').includes(f);
    });
    if (list.length === 0){
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-note">Sonuç bulunamadı. Aşağıdan yeni bir okul ekleyebilirsiniz.</div>';
      return;
    }
    el.innerHTML = list.map(s=>{
      const already = state.myPrefs.includes(s.id);
      return `<div class="school-row">
        <div>
          <div class="school-main">${escapeHtml(s.name)}</div>
          <div class="school-meta">${escapeHtml(s.city)} · ${escapeHtml(s.branch)}${s.hizmet ? ' · Hizmet Bölgesi/Alanı: '+escapeHtml(s.hizmet) : ''}${s.kod ? ' · Kod: '+escapeHtml(s.kod) : ''}</div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;">
          <span class="quota-pill">Kontenjan ${s.quota}</span>
          <button class="btn btn-sm ${already?'secondary':''}" data-add="${s.id}" ${already || state.myPrefs.length>=40 ? 'disabled':''}>${already?'Eklendi':'Tercih Et'}</button>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
    el.querySelectorAll('[data-add]').forEach(btn=>{
      btn.onclick = ()=> addPref(btn.getAttribute('data-add'));
    });
  }
  function escapeHtml(s){ return (s||'').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c])); }

  function setSchoolYearFilter(yil){
    if (state.schoolYearFilter === yil) return;
    state.schoolYearFilter = yil;
    $('yearToggle2024').classList.toggle('active', yil === 2024);
    $('yearToggle2023').classList.toggle('active', yil === 2023);
    $('yearToggle2022').classList.toggle('active', yil === 2022);
    $('ilSelect').value = '';
    renderIlSelect();
    renderIlceSelect('');
    renderSchoolList($('schoolSearch').value);
    updateMapSelection();
  }
  $('yearToggle2024').addEventListener('click', ()=> setSchoolYearFilter(2024));
  $('yearToggle2023').addEventListener('click', ()=> setSchoolYearFilter(2023));
  $('yearToggle2022').addEventListener('click', ()=> setSchoolYearFilter(2022));

  $('schoolSearch').addEventListener('input', (e)=> renderSchoolList(e.target.value));

  $('ilSelect').addEventListener('change', (e)=>{
    renderIlceSelect(e.target.value);
    renderSchoolList($('schoolSearch').value);
    updateMapSelection();
  });
  $('ilceSelect').addEventListener('change', ()=> renderSchoolList($('schoolSearch').value));

  function addPref(schoolId){
    if (state.myPrefs.includes(schoolId)) return;
    if (state.myPrefs.length >= 40){ toast('En fazla 40 tercih ekleyebilirsiniz.'); return; }
    state.myPrefs.push(schoolId);
    renderPrefList();
    renderSchoolList($('schoolSearch').value);
  }
  function removePref(schoolId){
    state.myPrefs = state.myPrefs.filter(id=>id!==schoolId);
    renderPrefList();
    renderSchoolList($('schoolSearch').value);
  }
  function movePref(schoolId, dir){
    const i = state.myPrefs.indexOf(schoolId);
    const j = i + dir;
    if (j<0 || j>=state.myPrefs.length) return;
    [state.myPrefs[i], state.myPrefs[j]] = [state.myPrefs[j], state.myPrefs[i]];
    renderPrefList();
  }

  function renderPrefList(){
    const ul = $('prefList');
    $('prefCount').textContent = state.myPrefs.length ? `(${state.myPrefs.length}/40)` : '';
    if (state.myPrefs.length === 0){
      ul.innerHTML=''; $('prefEmpty').style.display='block'; return;
    }
    $('prefEmpty').style.display='none';
    const byId = Object.fromEntries(state.schools.map(s=>[s.id,s]));
    ul.innerHTML = state.myPrefs.map((id, idx)=>{
      const s = byId[id];
      if (!s) return '';
      return `<li class="pref-item" draggable="true" data-id="${id}">
        <div class="pref-order">${idx+1}</div>
        <div style="flex:1;">
          <div class="pref-name">${escapeHtml(s.name)}</div>
          <div class="pref-meta">${escapeHtml(s.city)} · ${escapeHtml(s.branch)}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="pref-actions">
          <button class="icon-btn" data-up="${id}" title="Yukarı">↑</button>
          <button class="icon-btn" data-down="${id}" title="Aşağı">↓</button>
          <button class="icon-btn" data-remove="${id}" title="Kaldır">✕</button>
        </div>
      </li>`;
    }).join('');
    ul.querySelectorAll('[data-up]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>movePref(b.getAttribute('data-up'), -1));
    ul.querySelectorAll('[data-down]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>movePref(b.getAttribute('data-down'), 1));
    ul.querySelectorAll('[data-remove]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>removePref(b.getAttribute('data-remove')));
    initDragAndDrop(ul);
  }

  let dragSrcId = null;
  function initDragAndDrop(ul){
    const items = ul.querySelectorAll('.pref-item');
    items.forEach(item=>{
      item.addEventListener('dragstart', (e)=>{
        dragSrcId = item.getAttribute('data-id');
        item.classList.add('dragging');
        e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
        try{ e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', dragSrcId); }catch(err){}
      });
      item.addEventListener('dragend', ()=>{
        item.classList.remove('dragging');
        items.forEach(i=>i.classList.remove('drag-over'));
        dragSrcId = null;
      });
      item.addEventListener('dragover', (e)=>{
        e.preventDefault();
        e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';
        if (item.getAttribute('data-id') !== dragSrcId){
          items.forEach(i=>i.classList.remove('drag-over'));
          item.classList.add('drag-over');
        }
      });
      item.addEventListener('dragleave', ()=>{
        item.classList.remove('drag-over');
      });
      item.addEventListener('drop', (e)=>{
        e.preventDefault();
        item.classList.remove('drag-over');
        const targetId = item.getAttribute('data-id');
        if (!dragSrcId || dragSrcId === targetId) return;
        const from = state.myPrefs.indexOf(dragSrcId);
        const to = state.myPrefs.indexOf(targetId);
        if (from === -1 || to === -1) return;
        state.myPrefs.splice(from, 1);
        state.myPrefs.splice(to, 0, dragSrcId);
        renderPrefList();
      });
    });
  }

  $('backToStep0').onclick = ()=> goStep(0);
  $('toSimBtn').onclick = async ()=>{
    if (state.myPrefs.length === 0){ toast('En az bir tercih eklemelisiniz.'); return; }
    safeSetLocal('my-preferences', JSON.stringify({ schoolIds: state.myPrefs }));
    if (state.profile.score !== null){
      await upsertParticipant({
        id: state.profileId, name: state.profile.name || ('Aday-'+state.profileId.slice(0,4)),
        score: state.profile.score, preferences: state.myPrefs, updatedAt: Date.now()
      });
    }
    state.maxStepUnlocked = Math.max(state.maxStepUnlocked, 2);
    renderStepper();
    goStep(2);
    toast('Tercihleriniz kaydedildi.');
  };
  $('backToStep1').onclick = ()=> goStep(1);

  // ---------- STEP 3: SIMULATION ----------
  $('runSimBtn').onclick = async ()=>{
    if (state.profile.score === null){ toast('Önce puanınızı girmelisiniz.'); goStep(0); return; }
    if (state.myPrefs.length === 0){ toast('Önce okul tercihi eklemelisiniz.'); goStep(1); return; }

    $('runSimBtn').disabled = true;
    $('runSimBtn').textContent = 'Hesaplanıyor…';

    let participants = await getAllParticipants();
    // ensure current user is represented with latest local data
    participants = participants.filter(p=>p.id !== state.profileId);
    participants.push({
      id: state.profileId,
      name: state.profile.name || ('Aday-'+state.profileId.slice(0,4)),
      score: state.profile.score,
      preferences: state.myPrefs,
      updatedAt: Date.now()
    });

    // sort desc by score, tie-break by earlier updatedAt (submitted first)
    participants.sort((a,b)=> (b.score - a.score) || ((a.updatedAt||0) - (b.updatedAt||0)));

    const quotaLeft = {};
    state.schools.forEach(s=> quotaLeft[s.id] = Number(s.quota)||0);
    const byId = Object.fromEntries(state.schools.map(s=>[s.id,s]));

    const results = participants.map((p, idx)=>{
      let assignedId = null, prefRank = null;
      for (let i=0;i<(p.preferences||[]).length;i++){
        const sid = p.preferences[i];
        if (quotaLeft[sid] > 0){
          quotaLeft[sid]--; assignedId = sid; prefRank = i+1; break;
        }
      }
      return { rank: idx+1, ...p, assignedId, prefRank };
    });

    renderMyResult(results);
    renderResultsTable(results);
    await updatePoolInfo();

    $('runSimBtn').disabled = false;
    $('runSimBtn').textContent = 'Simülasyonu Yeniden Çalıştır';

    collapseIntoActionsBar();
  };

  function collapseIntoActionsBar(){
    const bar = $('simActionsBar');
    if (bar.classList.contains('show')) return; // already collapsed
    const intro = $('simIntroCard');
    intro.classList.add('collapsing');
    setTimeout(()=>{
      intro.style.display = 'none';
      const counter = $('simCounter');
      const btnGroup = document.createElement('div');
      btnGroup.className = 'sim-actions-buttons';
      btnGroup.appendChild($('runSimBtn'));
      btnGroup.appendChild($('backToStep1'));
      bar.appendChild(counter);
      bar.appendChild(btnGroup);
      bar.classList.add('show');
    }, 350);
  }

  function renderMyResult(results){
    const me = results.find(r=>r.id===state.profileId);
    const card = $('myResultCard');
    card.style.display = 'block';
    if (!me){ card.innerHTML=''; return; }
    const school = me.assignedId ? state.schools.find(s=>s.id===me.assignedId) : null;
    if (school){
      card.innerHTML = `
        <h2 class="section-title">Tahmini sonucunuz</h2>
        <table class="result-table">
          <caption>Yerleştirme Sonucu</caption>
          <tbody>
            <tr><td class="rlabel">Kurum</td><td class="rvalue">${escapeHtml(school.city)} - ${escapeHtml(school.name)}</td></tr>
            <tr><td class="rlabel">Tercih Sırası</td><td class="rvalue">${me.prefRank}</td></tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
        <div class="result-note">Bu simülasyonda, puan sıralamanız (<b>${me.rank}.</b>) ile mevcut havuzdaki diğer katılımcıların tercihlerine göre bu kuruma <b>${me.prefRank}.</b> tercihiniz olarak yerleşmiş görünüyorsunuz.</div>`;
    } else {
      card.innerHTML = `
        <h2 class="section-title">Tahmini sonucunuz</h2>
        <table class="result-table unplaced">
          <caption>Yerleştirme Sonucu</caption>
          <tbody>
            <tr><td class="rlabel">Kurum</td><td class="rvalue">Yerleşemedi</td></tr>
            <tr><td class="rlabel">Tercih Sırası</td><td class="rvalue">-</td></tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
        <div class="result-note">Mevcut havuz koşullarında, girdiğiniz ${state.myPrefs.length} tercihin hiçbirinde size kontenjan kalmamış görünüyor. Tercih listenize daha fazla veya daha düşük talep gören okul eklemeyi deneyebilirsiniz.</div>`;
    }
  }

  function renderResultsTable(results){
    const wrap = $('tableCard');
    wrap.style.display = 'block';
    const table = $('resultsTable');
    const rows = results.map(r=>{
      const school = r.assignedId ? state.schools.find(s=>s.id===r.assignedId) : null;
      const isMe = r.id === state.profileId;
      return `<tr class="${isMe?'me':''}">
        <td>${r.rank}</td>
        <td>${escapeHtml(r.name)}${isMe?' <span style="color:var(--accent-blue);">(siz)</span>':''}</td>
        <td>${fmtScore(r.score)}</td>
        <td>${school ? `<span class="placed">${escapeHtml(school.name)}<span class="placed-city">${escapeHtml(school.city)}</span></span>` : '<span class="unplaced">Yerleşemedi</span>'}</td>
        <td>${r.prefRank ?? '-'}</td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join('');
    table.innerHTML = `<thead><tr><th>Sıra</th><th>Aday</th><th>Puan</th><th>Atandığı Yer</th><th>Kaçıncı Tercih</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody>`;
  }

  init();
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
